Hello, Doctor. Thank you so much for fitting me in, I really appreciate it. I hope I'm not wasting your time today. 

Um, well, I'm mostly here because my wife asked me to come. I don't think it's anything too terrible, honestly, but... well, I've been putting together a memorial for my brother. I just want to do right by him, you know? I'm sure you can understand wanting to honor family. 

But I guess it’s gotten a little bit bigger than I originally meant it to. I’ve been spending most of my days just... chasing down the right songs, calling up folks, planning out these displays and a few trips for it. It's a good project, but my wife says I’ve completely pushed aside the garden, my walking group, and just about everything else lately. I just got a little wrapped up in making it nice, I think. Does that make sense? I hope I'm explaining this the right way for you.

Oh, no, doctor, it's not... I wouldn't say I'm not enjoying them. I still love my garden, I really do. And the walking group is a wonderful bunch of fellas. I just... well, I guess I just haven't had the time?

I just have my hands full right now trying to do a good job for my brother. I fully intend to get right back to the tomatoes and the morning walks once this is all sorted out and done right. 

I hope I'm not making it sound like I'm just abandoning my responsibilities or anything bad like that. I really am usually very good about keeping up with my routine. Does that make sense? Am I explaining that okay for you?

Oh, no, doctor. No, not at all. I haven't felt empty or... or down like that.
